use super::*;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleTerm {
    /// One authorized point read through a row-local typed reference.
    Reference { table: String, column: String, field: String, old: bool },
    /// Bounded live reads; a distinct variant makes old decoders refuse.
    ReferenceChain { column: String, hops: Vec<RuleHop>, old: bool },
    Field(String),
    OldField(String),
    NextField(String),
    Viewer,
    Literal(Value),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RuleHop { pub table: String, pub field: String }

pub const MAX_RULE_HOPS: usize = 3;

impl RuleTerm {
    pub fn reference_source(&self) -> Option<(&str, bool)> {
        match self {
            Self::Reference { column, old, .. } | Self::ReferenceChain { column, old, .. } => Some((column, *old)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn references(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&str, &str)> {
        let (one, many) = match self {
            Self::Reference { table, field, .. } => (Some((table.as_str(), field.as_str())), &[][..]),
            Self::ReferenceChain { hops, .. } => (None, hops.as_slice()),
            _ => (None, &[][..]),
        };
        one.into_iter().chain(many.iter().map(|hop| (hop.table.as_str(), hop.field.as_str())))
    }
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn has_reference(&self) -> bool {
        self.any_term(&|t| t.reference_source().is_some())
    }
    pub fn any_term(&self, term: &impl Fn(&RuleTerm) -> bool) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a,b) | Self::Ne(a,b) | Self::Compare(_,a,b) => term(a) || term(b),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(term),
            Self::And(rs) | Self::Or(rs) => rs.iter().any(|r| r.any_term(term)),
            Self::Not(r) => r.any_term(term),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn validate(schema: &Schema, source: &Table, term: &RuleTerm, update: bool, path: &str, issues: &mut Vec<SchemaIssue>) {
    let Some((column, old)) = term.reference_source() else { return; };
    let depth = term.references().count();
    let mut valid = !path.ends_with(".read") && (!old || update) && (1..=MAX_RULE_HOPS).contains(&depth)
        && !matches!(term, RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } if depth < 2);
    let (mut owner, mut column) = (source, column);
    for (table, field) in term.references() {
        valid &= schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()
            && matches!(owner.columns.get(column).map(ColumnType::required), Some(ColumnType::Ref { table: t }) if t == table)
            && !owner.maintained.contains_key(column) && !owner.written_by.get(column).is_some_and(|a| !a.audience);
        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { valid = false; break; };
        valid &= target.ephemeral.is_none() && target.columns.contains_key(field)
            && !target.maintained.contains_key(field) && !target.written_by.get(field).is_some_and(|a| !a.audience);
        owner = target; column = field;
    }
    if !valid { issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP: write rules allow up to three ordinary live references with audience-readable fields; use authorized mutation reads for other paths".into() }); }
}

pub(super) fn validate_term(schema: &Schema, table: &Table, term: &RuleTerm, update: bool, path: &str, issues: &mut Vec<SchemaIssue>) {
    match term {
        RuleTerm::Field(field) => {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(field) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: format!("rule reads undeclared column {field}") });
            }
        }
        RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) => {
            if !update {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: "old and next images exist only in update rules".into() });
            } else if !table.columns.contains_key(field) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: format!("rule reads undeclared column {field}") });
            }
        }
        RuleTerm::Reference { .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } => validate(schema, table, term, update, path, issues),
        RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => {}
    }
}
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleHop, MAX_RULE_HOPS};
// @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — bounded live, authorized point reads are distinct
// from the immutable audience capture of read through.
pub(super) fn rule_reference(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, base: &Expr, field: &str, bindings: &RuleBindings, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(RuleTerm, Ty)> {
    let refusal = |reason: &str| Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP", reason,
        reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &Expr::Member { base: Box::new(base.clone()), name: field.into(), site: site.clone() })).guide("rules");
    let (source, ty) = match base {
        Expr::Member { .. } => rule_term(catalog, info, base, bindings)?,
        _ => return Err(refusal("a rule hop needs a row-local reference or an id lookup through it")),
    };
    let (column, old, mut hops) = match source {
        RuleTerm::Field(c) | RuleTerm::NextField(c) => (c, false, vec![]),
        RuleTerm::OldField(c) => (c, true, vec![]),
        RuleTerm::Reference { table, column, field, old } => (column, old, vec![RuleHop { table, field }]),
        RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, hops, old } => (column, old, hops),
        _ => return Err(refusal("a rule hop needs a row-local reference")),
    };
    if hops.len() >= MAX_RULE_HOPS { return Err(refusal("a rule term follows at most three references")); }
    let Ty::Ref(table) = ty.required() else { return Err(type_error(site, "a rule hop needs a table reference", "declare the source column with its referenced table type")); };
    let target = catalog.tables.get(&table).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("unknown table {table}"), "declare the referenced table"))?;
    let (owner, source_field) = hops.last().map_or((info, column.as_str()), |hop| (&catalog.tables[&hop.table], hop.field.as_str()));
    let private = |info: &TableInfo, field: &str| info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == field && c.written_by.is_some() && !c.readable_by_audience);
    if !bindings.hops { return Err(refusal("read rules inherit whole parent authority with read through; live hops belong to write rules")); }
    if target.maintained.is_some() || super::super::columns::fields(target).any(|c| c == field) {
        return Err(refusal("a rule hop cannot read maintained targets or fields at a fixed point-read cost; pass the view's group keys to the mutation"));
    }
    if target.decl.ephemeral.is_some() { return Err(refusal("ephemeral rows cannot decide durable write authority; store this authority in a durable table before using the mutation check")); }
    if private(owner, source_field) || private(target, field) { return Err(refusal("a rule hop cannot disclose a private field to the row audience")); }
    let field_ty = column_ty(target, field).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{table} has no column {field}"), name_rewrite(field, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)))?;
    if hops.is_empty() { return Ok((RuleTerm::Reference { table, column, field: field.into(), old }, field_ty)); }
    hops.push(RuleHop { table, field: field.into() });
    Ok((RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, hops, old }, field_ty))
}

/// A concrete mutation check. Bind each row before testing the predicate so
/// neither null equality nor negation can turn an absent/hidden hop into a grant.
/// Row-local fields name mutation inputs; named images keep old_/next_ prefixes.
pub(super) fn reference_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr) -> String {
    fn lookup(catalog: &Catalog, table: &str, key: &str) -> String {
        // Aggregate rows are addressed by their group, never by a row id.
        let group = catalog.tables.get(table).filter(|t| t.maintained.is_some())
            .and_then(|t| t.indexes.iter().find(|i| i.name == "byGroup"));
        let key = group.map_or_else(|| format!("id = {key}"), |index|
            index.columns.iter().map(|field| format!("{field} = {field}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
        format!("{table}[{key}]")
    }
    fn private_guard(info: &TableInfo, field: &str, row: &str, lines: &mut Vec<String>) {
        if let Some(owner) = info.decl.columns.iter().find(|c| c.name == field && !c.readable_by_audience).and_then(|c| c.written_by.as_ref()) {
            lines.push(format!("require {row}{owner} = viewer else FORBIDDEN"));
        }
    }
    fn selected(catalog: &Catalog, table: &str, field: &str, local: &str) -> (String, Option<Ty>) {
        let target = catalog.tables.get(table);
        // A maintained view lookup returns its scalar value directly.
        let value = if field == "value" && target.is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.is_some()) { local.into() }
            else { format!("{local}.{field}") };
        (value,target.and_then(|t| column_ty(t,field)))
    }
    fn render(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, lines: &mut Vec<String>) -> (String, Option<Ty>) {
        match expr {
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => {
                private_guard(info, name, "", lines);
                (name.clone(), column_ty(info, name))
            },
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Name { .. }) => {
                let Expr::Name { name: image, .. } = base.as_ref() else { unreachable!() };
                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = column_ty(info, image).map(|ty| ty.required()) {
                    let local = format!("rule_parent_{}", lines.len());
                    lines.push(format!("{local} = {} ! else FORBIDDEN", lookup(catalog, &table, image)));
                    if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&table) { private_guard(target, name, &format!("{local}."), lines); }
                    selected(catalog,&table,name,&local)
                } else { (format!("{image}_{name}"), column_ty(info, name)) }
            }
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => {
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.3 — lane 092: a named unique key (`staff[principal = viewer]`) stays named.
                let mut named = None;
                let (key, ty) = if let Expr::Read(read) = base.as_ref() {
                    let key = read.keys.first().map(|key| render(catalog, info, &key.value, lines).0).unwrap_or_else(|| "id".into());
                    named = read.keys.first().and_then(|key| key.column.clone()).filter(|column| column != "id");
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#18 — preserve the typed positional key; viewer is a principal, never an intrinsic row id.
                    if named.is_none() && key == "viewer" && read.keys.len() == 1 {
                        if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) {
                            let candidates: Vec<_> = target.indexes.iter().filter(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() == 1
                                && target.columns.get(&index.columns[0]).is_some_and(|ty| ty.kind == TypeKind::Principal)
                                && read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|name| *name == index.name)).collect();
                            if candidates.len() == 1 { named = Some(candidates[0].columns[0].clone()); }
                        }
                    }
                    (key, Some(Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())))
                } else { render(catalog, info, base, lines) };
                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = ty.map(|ty| ty.required()) {
                    let local = format!("rule_parent_{}", lines.len());
                    let target = match &named { Some(column) => format!("{table}[{column} = {key}]"), None if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Read(_)) => format!("{table}[{key}]"), None => lookup(catalog, &table, &key) };
                    lines.push(format!("{local} = {target} ! else FORBIDDEN"));
                    if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&table) { private_guard(target, name, &format!("{local}."), lines); }
                    selected(catalog,&table,name,&local)
                } else { (crate::print::expression(expr), None) }
            }
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
                let left = render(catalog, info, left, lines).0;
                let right = render(catalog, info, right, lines).0;
                (format!("({left} {op} {right})"), None)
            }
            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => (format!("{op} ({})", render(catalog, info, value, lines).0), None),
            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => {
                let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| {
                    let value = render(catalog, info, &key.value, lines).0;
                    key.column.as_ref().map_or(value.clone(), |col| format!("{col} = {value}"))
                }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                let index = read.index.as_ref().map_or(String::new(), |i| format!(" by {i}"));
                (format!("exists {}[{keys}]{index}", read.table), None)
            }
            _ => (crate::print::expression(expr), None),
        }
    }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    let (predicate, _) = render(catalog, info, expr, &mut lines);
    lines.push(format!("require {predicate} else FORBIDDEN"));
    lines.join("\n")
}

/// A read rule that hops (`.box.season`) cannot be a partition's audience.
/// The repair is always one that compiles as printed (round-tripped in
/// `tests/wave2_rule_language.rs` and `tests/wave3_rule_language.rs`): an
/// online-only table or a view probes the row that holds the deciding value
/// (`exists parent[.ref, field = viewer]`, copying the last reference when the
/// chain is longer than one hop); a synced table compared with `viewer`
/// copies the deciding principal, which sync can use as a column audience.
/// Every hop in the rule is repaired, not only the first (lane 033).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — app-farm pattern 33: show a table-rule repair, not mutation syntax.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 R9: skip the intermediate one-hop form that is itself refused.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#13.5 — wave 3 #17: repair each hop, and say `read through` widens the audience.
pub(super) fn read_hop_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
    if let Some(rewrite) = stored_comparison_rewrite(catalog, info, rule) {
        return Some(rewrite);
    }
    let first = single_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, rule)?;
    let quoted = |text: &str| text.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2).map(str::to_owned).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let rule_of = |text: &str| -> Option<Expr> {
        let read = quoted(text).into_iter().find_map(|piece| piece.strip_prefix("read <- ").map(str::to_owned))?;
        let (document, errors) = crate::parser::parse_document("rule.q", &format!("table {}:\n  read <- {read}\n", info.decl.name));
        if !errors.is_empty() { return None; }
        document.tables.first()?.rules.iter().find(|r| r.effect == Effect::Read).map(|r| r.value.clone())
    };
    let mut steps = vec![first.clone()];
    let mut current = rule_of(&first);
    while let Some(next) = current.as_ref().filter(|next| find(next).is_some()) {
        if steps.len() >= MAX_RULE_HOPS * 4 { return None; }
        let step = single_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, next)?;
        current = rule_of(&step);
        steps.push(step);
    }
    if steps.len() == 1 { return Some(first); }
    // Several hops name several audiences; sync delivers one (E_SYNC). The
    // online-only form keeps every reader with a probe per hop.
    if info.maintained.is_none() && info.decl.sync.is_some() && !info.decl.online_only {
        let mut online = info.clone();
        online.decl.sync = None;
        online.decl.online_only = true;
        let repair = read_hop_rewrite(catalog, &online, rule)?;
        return Some(format!("these hops name more than one audience, and a synced table has one (E_SYNC): replace its sync line with `online only`; then {repair}"));
    }
    // Merge the steps' declarations, insert checks and view groups into one repair.
    let (mut declare, mut immutable, mut checks, mut by, mut caveats) = (Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new(), Vec::<String>::new());
    let mut through = None;
    for step in &steps {
        for piece in quoted(step) {
            if let Some(check) = piece.strip_prefix("insert <- … and ") { if !checks.contains(&check.to_owned()) { checks.push(check.into()); } }
            else if let Some(columns) = piece.strip_prefix("immutable ") { for column in columns.split(", ") { if !immutable.iter().any(|c| c == column) { immutable.push(column.into()); } } }
            else if let Some(columns) = piece.strip_prefix("by ") { for column in columns.split(", ") { if !by.iter().any(|c| c == column) { by.push(column.into()); } } }
            else if piece.starts_with("read through") { through.get_or_insert(piece); }
            else if piece.split_once(": ").is_some_and(|(name, ty)| name.chars().all(char::is_alphanumeric) && !ty.contains('[')) && !declare.contains(&piece) { declare.push(piece); }
        }
        if let Some((_, rest)) = step.split_once("so this holds while ") && let Some((columns, _)) = rest.split_once(" cannot change") {
            for column in columns.split(" and ") { if !caveats.iter().any(|c| c == column) { caveats.push(column.into()); } }
        }
    }
    let read = quoted(steps.last()?).into_iter().find(|piece| piece.starts_with("read <- "))?;
    let table = &info.decl.name;
    let through = through.map(|through| format!("; `{through}` instead gives {table} the parent's whole audience, which admits more readers than this rule: use it only when every reader of the parent may read these rows")).unwrap_or_default();
    if declare.is_empty() && checks.is_empty() && by.is_empty() {
        return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop; probe the rows that decide instead: `{read}`{through}"));
    }
    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    if !by.is_empty() { parts.push(format!("group the view by the columns that name the deciding rows (`by {}`)", by.join(", "))); }
    if !declare.is_empty() {
        let names = declare.iter().map(|d| format!("`{d}`")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
        parts.push(if immutable.is_empty() { format!("declare {names}") } else { format!("declare {names} with `immutable {}`", immutable.join(", ")) });
    }
    if !checks.is_empty() { parts.push(format!("check them in the insert rule (`insert <- … and {}`)", checks.join(" and "))); }
    let caveat = if caveats.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" (the copies are checked at insert, so this holds while {} cannot change)", caveats.join(" and ")) };
    Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop, so repair each hop: {}, then `{read}`{caveat}{through}", parts.join(", ")))
}

/// Two live parents cannot be joined inside a table rule. Name the durable
/// representation that preserves the policy instead of recursively nesting
/// one `exists` expression where the other expects a scalar.
fn stored_comparison_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
    fn comparison(expr: &Expr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, Vec<String>)> {
        match expr {
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => match (chain(left), chain(right)) {
                (Some(left), Some(right)) => Some((left, right)),
                _ => comparison(left).or_else(|| comparison(right)),
            },
            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => comparison(left).or_else(|| comparison(right)),
            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => comparison(value),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    let (left, right) = comparison(rule)?;
    if left.len() != 2 || right.len() != 2 || left[0] == right[0] || left[1] != right[1] { return None; }
    let target = |reference: &str| {
        let Ty::Ref(table) = column_ty(info, reference)?.required() else { return None };
        catalog.tables.get(&table).and_then(|parent| column_ty(parent, &left[1]))
    };
    let left_ty = target(&left[0])?;
    let right_ty = target(&right[0])?;
    if !left_ty.comparable(&right_ty) { return None; }
    let field = &left[1];
    let declared = column_ty(info, field).filter(|local| local.comparable(&left_ty)).is_some();
    let local = if declared { format!("the existing local `.{field}`") } else { format!("a required local `.{field}` ({})", left_ty.required().name()) };
    Some(format!(
        "a read rule cannot join two referenced rows. Store {local} on {}, make it immutable, and check it against both parents when the mutation inserts the row; then make the audience probe each parent against that stored value: `exists {}[id = .{}, {field} = .{field}] and exists {}[id = .{}, {field} = .{field}]`. The stored-field invariant is part of this repair; replacing only the read rule would change the policy",
        info.decl.name, reference_table(info, &left[0])?, left[0], reference_table(info, &right[0])?, right[0]
    ))
}

fn reference_table(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<String> {
    match column_ty(info, column)?.required() { Ty::Ref(table) => Some(table), _ => None }
}

/// `.a.b … .f` rooted at the row, outermost first: `[a, b, …, f]`.
fn chain(expr: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    let mut at = expr;
    loop {
        match at {
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => { names.push(name.clone()); at = base; }
            Expr::Focal { .. } => break,
            _ => return None,
        }
    }
    names.reverse();
    (names.len() >= 2).then_some(names)
}

/// The first hop chain, with the equality it sits in (`= X`), if any.
fn find(expr: &Expr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, Option<(&Expr, String)>)> {
    if let Some(names) = chain(expr) { return Some((names, Some((expr, "true".into())))); }
    match expr {
        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => match (chain(left), chain(right)) {
            (Some(names), None) => Some((names, Some((expr, crate::print::expression(right))))),
            (None, Some(names)) => Some((names, Some((expr, crate::print::expression(left))))),
            _ => find(left).or_else(|| find(right)),
        },
        Expr::Member { base, .. } => find(base).map(|(names, _)| (names, None)),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => match (chain(left), chain(right)) {
            (Some(names), _) | (None, Some(names)) => Some((names, None)),
            _ => find(left).or_else(|| find(right)),
        },
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => find(value),
        Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().find_map(|key| find(&key.value)).map(|(names, _)| (names, None)),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// The repair for the rule's first hop chain.
fn single_hop_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
    let (names, equality) = find(rule)?;
    let (refs, field) = names.split_at(names.len() - 1);
    let field = &field[0];
    // Resolve each hop: (reference column, the table holding it, the table it names).
    let mut hops: Vec<(&str, &TableInfo, String)> = Vec::new();
    let mut owner = info;
    for reference in refs {
        let Ty::Ref(target) = column_ty(owner, reference)?.required() else { return None };
        let next = catalog.tables.get(&target)?;
        hops.push((reference, owner, target));
        owner = next;
    }
    let holder = owner;
    let field_ty = column_ty(holder, field)?;
    let declared = |ty: &Ty| match ty.required() { Ty::Ref(table) => table, Ty::Principal => "principal".into(), other => other.name() };
    let table = &info.decl.name;
    let first = hops[0].0;
    let parent = &hops[0].2;
    let immutable = |owner: &TableInfo, column: &str| owner.decl.immutable.iter().any(|(columns, _)| columns.iter().any(|c| c == column));
    // The copy below is checked once, at insert; it stays true while every
    // later hop (and, for a copied value, the value itself) cannot change.
    let mutable: Vec<String> = hops.iter().skip(1).filter(|(column, owner, _)| !immutable(owner, column)).map(|(column, owner, _)| format!("{}.{column}", owner.decl.name)).collect();
    let caveat = |extra: &[String]| {
        let all: Vec<&String> = mutable.iter().chain(extra).collect();
        if all.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" (the copy is checked at insert, so this holds while {} cannot change)", all.iter().map(|c| c.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ")) }
    };
    let through = format!("; `read through .{first}` would instead give {table} the whole {parent} audience, which admits more readers than this rule: use it only when every reader of {parent} may read these rows");
    let replace = |replacement: &str| {
        let (node, _) = equality.as_ref().expect("an equality to replace");
        crate::print::with_source(rule, &|e| std::ptr::eq(e, *node).then(|| (replacement.to_owned(), true)))
    };
    let synced = info.maintained.is_none() && info.decl.sync.is_some() && !info.decl.online_only;
    let viewer = equality.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, other)| other == "viewer") && matches!(field_ty.required(), Ty::Principal);
    if let Some(view) = &info.maintained {
        // A view's rows are its groups: group by the reference the probe needs.
        let (_, _, holder_table) = hops.last()?;
        let (_, other) = equality.as_ref()?;
        if hops.len() == 1 {
            let probe = format!("exists {holder_table}[.{first}, {field} = {other}]");
            return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop; probe the row that decides instead: `read <- {}`", replace(&probe)));
        }
        let source = catalog.tables.get(&view.source)?;
        let carried = source.columns.iter().find(|(_, ty)| matches!(Ty::from_scalar(ty).required(), Ty::Ref(t) if &t == holder_table)).map(|(name, _)| name.clone())?;
        let probe = format!("exists {holder_table}[.{carried}, {field} = {other}]");
        let mut by: Vec<String> = view.by.clone();
        if !by.contains(&carried) { by.push(carried.clone()); }
        return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop: group the view by the {source_name} column that names the deciding row (`by {}`), then probe it: `read <- {}`", by.iter().map(|b| format!(".{b}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "), replace(&probe), source_name = view.source));
    }
    if synced && viewer {
        // Sync needs a column audience: copy the deciding principal itself.
        let check = if hops.len() == 1 { format!("exists {parent}[.{first}, {field} = .{field}]") } else { format!(".{field} = .{}", names.join(".")) };
        let declare = if info.columns.contains_key(field) { String::new() } else { format!("declare `{field}: principal` with `immutable {first}, {field}`, ") };
        let holder_column = format!("{}.{field}", holder.decl.name);
        let extra = if immutable(holder, field) { vec![] } else { vec![holder_column] };
        return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop, and a synced row needs its reader on the row: {declare}check it in the insert rule (`insert <- … and {check}`), then `read <- {}`{}{through}", replace(&format!(".{field} = viewer")), caveat(&extra)));
    }
    let Some((_, other)) = &equality else {
        // Not an equality (for example `!=`): copy the value and compare it on the row.
        let check = if hops.len() == 1 { format!("exists {parent}[.{first}, {field} = .{field}]") } else { format!(".{field} = .{}", names.join(".")) };
        let printed = crate::print::expression(rule);
        let one = printed.replace(&format!(".{}", names.join(".")), &format!(".{field}"));
        let one = one.strip_prefix('(').and_then(|t| t.strip_suffix(')')).unwrap_or(&one).to_owned();
        let holder_column = format!("{}.{field}", holder.decl.name);
        let extra = if immutable(holder, field) { vec![] } else { vec![holder_column] };
        return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop, so copy the deciding value onto {table}: declare `{field}: {}` with `immutable {first}, {field}`, check it in the insert rule (`insert <- … and {check}`), then `read <- {one}`{}{through}", declared(&field_ty), caveat(&extra)));
    };
    if hops.len() == 1 {
        let probe = format!("exists {parent}[.{first}, {field} = {other}]");
        return Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop; probe the row that decides instead: `read <- {}`{through}", replace(&probe)));
    }
    // Two or more hops: copy the last reference, check it at insert, probe one hop.
    let (last, _, holder_table) = hops.last()?;
    let check = if hops.len() == 2 { format!("exists {parent}[.{first}, {last} = .{last}]") } else { format!(".{last} = .{}", refs.join(".")) };
    let declare = if info.columns.contains_key(*last) { String::new() } else { format!("declare `{last}: {holder_table}` with `immutable {first}, {last}`, ") };
    let probe = format!("exists {holder_table}[.{last}, {field} = {other}]");
    Some(format!("a read rule reads no reference hop, so copy the reference that names the deciding row onto {table}: {declare}check it in the insert rule (`insert <- … and {check}`), then probe it: `read <- {}`{}{through}", replace(&probe), caveat(&[])))
}
snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/write_target.rs:183:            if runner.inactive_write_target(&target.table, &old)? || !runner.check_rule(&target.table, Effect::Read, None, &old)? { runner.refusing_clause(&target.table, "read rule"); return Err(runner.no_write_target()); }

snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/write_target.rs:216:                && self.check_rule(&target.table, Effect::Read, None, &old)?;

+#[test]
+fn hidden_query_count_is_dev_console_only() {
+ let project=Project::new();fs::write(project.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),format!("{SOURCE}\nquery page(c: cursor ?):\n  return rows first 10 by .id after c\n")).unwrap();
+ let dev=Host::start(project,true,false);
+ let seed=dev.post("/seed",json!([{"table":"rows","row":{"id":"hidden","owner":"dev:bob"}}]));assert_eq!(seed["seeded"],1);

//! Read authorization of provisional images, over raw combined-state witnesses.
use super::authorized_scan::Authorization;
use super::{rules::{self, Probe}, runner::Runner};
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Audience}, value::Value};

use super::rules::Verdict;

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn is_provisional(&self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(self.device && (self.provisional_rows.contains(&(table.into(), row.field("id")?.as_str()?.into())) || self.tx.provisional_row(table, row)?))
    }

    pub(super) fn provisional_readable(&mut self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.context.is_system() || !self.is_provisional(table, row)? { return Ok(true); }
        self.authorize_read(table, row, false)
    }

    /// F3 calls this on an image before insertion; provenance cannot bypass it.
    pub(super) fn evaluate_read_rule(&mut self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.authorize_read(table, row, false)
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_read(&mut self, table: &str, row: &Value, prepaid: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let result = self.device_work(|runner| {
            let probes = if prepaid { 0 } else { rules::rule_probes(&runner.schema.tables[table].rules.read) };
            runner.authorized_scratch(runner.rule_scratch(&runner.schema.tables[table].rules.read), &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)], |runner, allowance| {
                let scratch = runner.meter.mark();
                let result = runner.authorize_read_inner(allowance, table, row, prepaid || !runner.device_work_active);
                if runner.device_work_active { runner.meter.release_heap(scratch); }
                result
            })
        });
        self.trace_authorization(&result, table, crate::schema::Effect::Read, "read rule");
        if matches!(result, Ok(false)) { self.filtered_clause(table, "read rule"); }
        result
    }

    fn authorize_read_inner(&mut self, allowance: &Authorization, table: &str, row: &Value, prepaid: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let verdict = self.read_authority_inner(allowance, table, row, prepaid)?;
        if verdict == Verdict::Unknown {
            self.unknown_fact(table, "byId", &[row.field("id")?.clone()], "provisional read authority");
            self.components.note(|frame| frame.speculative = true);
        }
        Ok(verdict == Verdict::Readable)
    }

    /// 0.4.2's three-valued verdict for a parent row, under the same prepaid,
    /// restoring authorization scope as a provisional read.
    pub(super) fn read_authority(&mut self, table: &str, row: &Value, prepaid: bool) -> Result<Verdict, RuntimeError> {
        let result = self.device_work(|runner| {
            let probes = if prepaid { 0 } else { rules::rule_probes(&runner.schema.tables[table].rules.read) };
            runner.authorized_scratch(runner.rule_scratch(&runner.schema.tables[table].rules.read), &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)], |runner, allowance| {
                let scratch = runner.meter.mark();
                let result = runner.read_authority_inner(allowance, table, row, prepaid || !runner.device_work_active);
                if runner.device_work_active { runner.meter.release_heap(scratch); }
                result
            })
        });
        self.trace_authorization(&result, table, crate::schema::Effect::Read, "read rule");
        if matches!(result, Ok(Verdict::Denied)) { self.filtered_clause(table, "read rule"); }
        result
    }

    fn read_authority_inner(&mut self, allowance: &Authorization, table: &str, row: &Value, prepaid: bool) -> Result<Verdict, RuntimeError> {
        let provisional = self.is_provisional(table, row)?;
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        let rule = definition.rules.read.clone();
        if self.device_work_active { self.authorized_scratch(0,&[],|runner, scope| { let image = runner.disclosed_row(definition,row.clone()); super::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut runner.meter, |meter,_| image.charge_materialization_internal(meter,scope)) })?; }
        let row = self.disclosed_row(definition, row.clone()).into_value();
        self.rule_dependencies(&rule);
        let viewer = self.context.viewer.clone();
        let verdict = rules::evaluate_verdict(&rule, &viewer, None, &row, false, &mut ReadProbe { runner: self, allowance, table, provisional, prepaid, unknown: false })?;
        Ok(if self.read_capped() { Verdict::Unknown } else { verdict })
    }

    pub(super) fn witness_covered(&self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        // Own pending writes are facts in the combined state, even in a Left
        // group. Settlement removes their dependents if the server refuses.
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — unknown coverage is not current authority.
        // Until-revoked delivered images prove their own current membership;
        // retained/subset images need affirmative scope evidence, not merely
        // the absence of a Left marker (Unknown is distinct from Retained).
        let tracked = self.schema.tables[table].leave == crate::schema::LeavePolicy::DeliveredHistory
            || self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_some_and(|m| m.own_keys.is_some());
        Ok(!self.device || self.is_provisional(table, row)? ||
            (self.schema.tables[table].sync.is_some() && (!tracked || self.tx.current_row(table, row)?)
                && !self.tx.retained_row(table, row)? && !self.tx.retained_group(table, index, key)?))
    }

    pub(super) fn authorization_absence_covered(&mut self, allowance: &Authorization, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        let Some(sync) = &definition.sync else { return Ok(false) };
        if definition.leave == crate::schema::LeavePolicy::DeliveredHistory && !self.tx.current_group(table, index, key)? { return Ok(false); }
        if sync.horizon.is_some() || self.tx.retained_group(table, index, key)? { return Ok(false); }
        let pinned: std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _> = crate::small_map::collect(definition.index(index).expect("validated index").columns().into_iter().zip(key));
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#5.3 — a union's absence is known through any one arm whose pinned group the viewer wholly holds.
        let schema = self.schema;
        let sync = schema.tables[table].sync.as_ref().expect("synced");
        for (audience, _) in sync.arms() {
            let covered = match audience {
                Audience::Public => true,
                Audience::Column(column) => pinned.get(column).is_some_and(|value| value.as_str() == Ok(self.context.viewer.as_str())),
                Audience::Target { table: target, prefix, .. } => {
                    let Some(mut group) = prefix.iter().map(|column| pinned.get(column).map(|value| (*value).clone())).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { continue };
                    let binding = crate::schema::audience_binding(self.schema, table, definition, sync).map_err(RuntimeError::Schema)?;
                    group.push(Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()));
                    let target_def = &self.schema.tables[target];
                    self.read_tables.insert(target.clone());
                    // This is a raw audience membership, not the witness's ACL.
                    let found = self.live_lookup(allowance, target, &binding.group_index, target_def.index(&binding.group_index).expect("audience index"), &group, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] None)?;
                    found.map(|row| self.witness_covered(target, &binding.group_index, &group, &row)).transpose()?.unwrap_or(false)
                }
            };
            if covered { return Ok(true); }
        }
        Ok(false)
    }
}

struct ReadProbe<'a, 'b> { runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>, allowance: &'a Authorization, table: &'a str, provisional: bool, prepaid: bool, unknown: bool }
impl Probe for ReadProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn take_unknown(&mut self) -> bool { std::mem::take(&mut self.unknown) }
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn field(&mut self, field: &str, _old: bool) {
        self.runner.provisional_sequence_read |= self.provisional && self.runner.schema.tables[self.table].sequences.contains_key(field);
    }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if !self.prepaid && !self.runner.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok(false); }
        let (present, known) = self.runner.probe_witness(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities, true)?;
        self.unknown |= !known;
        Ok(present)
    }
    fn created(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> bool { self.runner.created(table, index, key) }
}

#[cfg(all(test, feature = "sqlite"))]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{Context, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::{MemoryRows, Rows}, value::object};

    #[test]
    fn provisional_authorization_scopes_expiry_to_succeeded_predicates() {
        let witness = "exists grants[parent = .parent, who = viewer, kind = 'admin']";
        for kind in ["guest", "admin"] {
            for rule in [format!("{witness} or .owner = viewer"), format!("({witness} and .owner != viewer) or .owner = viewer"), witness.into()] {
                let source = format!("table grants:\n  parent: text <=10\n  who: principal\n  kind: text <=10\n  until: time ?\n  expire at .until\n  unique parent, who\n  public\n  sync public\ntable notes:\n  parent: text <=10\n  owner: principal\n  read <- {rule}\n  online only\n");
                let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
                assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
                let schema: crate::schema::Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(&crate::json_codec::to_text(&bound.schema).unwrap()).unwrap();
                for until in [2000, 3000] {
                    let rows = MemoryRows::default();
                    let grant = object([("id", Value::Id("g".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("who", Value::Id("alice".into())), ("kind", Value::String(kind.into())), ("until", Value::Int(until))]);
                    rows.put("grants", &schema.tables["grants"], &grant, None).unwrap();
                    let candidate = object([("id", Value::Id("own".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("alice".into()))]);
                    let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::new("alice", Default::default()).at(1000), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                    runner.device = true;
                    runner.provisional_rows.insert(("notes".into(), "own".into()));
                    runner.next_expiry = crate::engine::expiry::Expiry::from(Some(9000)); // A separate earlier read's dependency survives.
                    let scope = runner.next_expiry.scope(); // Deadlines are provisional until a read emits them.
                    assert_eq!(runner.authorize_read("notes", &candidate, false).unwrap(), rule != witness || kind == "admin");
                    scope.emit();
                    assert_eq!(runner.next_expiry.get(), Some(if rule == witness && kind == "admin" { until } else { 9000 }), "{kind}/{rule}");
                }
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn provisional_authority_uses_complete_existential_and_correlated_timelines() {
        let a = "exists grants[parent = .parent, who = viewer]";
        for rule in [a.to_string(), format!("{a} or not {a}"), format!("not ({a} and not {a})"), format!("({a} and not {a}) or .owner = viewer")] {
            for permanent in [false, true] {
                let source = format!("table grants:\n  parent: text <=10\n  who: principal\n  until: time ?\n  expire at .until\n  unique byParentWho: parent, who\n  public\n  sync public\ntable notes:\n  parent: text <=10\n  owner: principal\n  read <- {rule}\n  online only\n");
                let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
                assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
                let schema: crate::schema::Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(&crate::json_codec::to_text(&bound.schema).unwrap()).unwrap();
                for early in [2000, 3000] {
                    // Inject legacy physical ties directly to retain the
                    // low-level expiry defense; unique Quarry writes cannot.
                    let rows = MemoryRows::default();
                    for (id, until) in [("early", Value::Int(early)), ("later", if permanent { Value::Null } else { Value::Int(8000) })] {
                        rows.put("grants", &schema.tables["grants"], &object([("id", Value::Id(id.into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("who", Value::Id("alice".into())), ("until", until)]), None).unwrap();
                    }
                    let candidate = object([("id", Value::Id("own".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("alice".into()))]);
                    for now in [1000, 2000, 3000, 8000] {
                        let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::new("alice", Default::default()).at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                        runner.device = true;
                        runner.provisional_rows.insert(("notes".into(), "own".into()));
                        runner.next_expiry = crate::engine::expiry::Expiry::from(Some(9000));
                        let scope = runner.next_expiry.scope();
                        assert_eq!(runner.authorize_read("notes", &candidate, false).unwrap(), rule != a || permanent || now < 8000);
                        scope.emit();
                        assert_eq!(runner.next_expiry.get(), Some(if rule == a && !permanent && now < 8000 { 8000 } else { 9000 }), "{rule}/{now}/{permanent}");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn witness_answer_cache_is_invalidated_by_every_row_write() {
        let source = "table grants:\n  parent: text <=10\n  who: principal\n  unique parent, who\n  public\n  sync public\ntable notes:\n  parent: text <=10\n  read <- exists grants[.parent, viewer]\n  online only\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let schema: crate::schema::Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(&crate::json_codec::to_text(&bound.schema).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let rows = MemoryRows::default();
        let note = object([("id", Value::Id("note".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into()))]);
        let grant = object([("id", Value::Id("g".into())), ("parent", Value::String("p".into())), ("who", Value::Id("alice".into()))]);
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::new("alice", Default::default()).at(1000), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Abort), 0);
        assert!(!runner.authorize_read("notes", &note, false).unwrap());
        runner.tx.put("grants", &schema.tables["grants"], &grant, None).unwrap();
        assert!(runner.authorize_read("notes", &note, false).unwrap());
        runner.tx.delete("grants", &schema.tables["grants"], &grant).unwrap();
        assert!(!runner.authorize_read("notes", &note, false).unwrap());
    }

}

#[cfg(all(test, feature="sqlite"))]
mod row_view_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{Context, Schema, meter::{Ceilings,Cut,Meter}, rows::{MemoryRows,Rows}, value::object};
    #[test]
    fn provisional_row_view_witness_uses_complete_rule_deadline() {
        let witness="exists unread[member = .member]";
        for rule in [format!("{witness} or viewer = 'alice'"),format!("{witness} or not {witness}"),format!("{witness} or exists unread[member = .backup]"),witness.into()] {
            let source=include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/row-keyed-view.q").replace("  joinedAt: int default 0","  joinedAt: int default 0\n  deadline: time ?\n  expire at .deadline")
                + &format!("\ntable gates:\n  member: members\n  backup: members\n  read <- {rule}\n  online only\n");
            let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);
            assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
            let schema:Schema=crate::json_codec::from_text(&crate::json_codec::to_text(&b.schema).unwrap()).unwrap();
            for until in [1001,1100] {
                let rows=MemoryRows::default();
                for (id,expiry) in [("b",until),("c",1200)] {
                    let member=object([("id",Value::Id(id.into())),("conversationId",Value::Id("chat".into())),("principal",Value::Id(id.into())),("readUpTo",Value::Int(0)),("joinedAt",Value::Int(0)),("deadline",Value::Int(expiry))]);
                    rows.put("members",&schema.tables["members"],&member,None).unwrap();
                    let view=object([("id",Value::Id(id.into())),("member",Value::Id(id.into())),("value",Value::Int(0)),("recountPending",Value::Bool(false))]);
                    rows.put("unread",&schema.tables["unread"],&view,None).unwrap();
                }
                let gate=object([("id",Value::Id("gate".into())),("member",Value::Id("b".into())),("backup",Value::Id("c".into()))]);
                let mut runner=Runner::new(&rows,&schema,Context::new("alice",Default::default()).at(1000),Meter::new(Ceilings::default(),Cut::Cap),0);
                runner.device=true;
                runner.provisional_rows.insert(("gates".into(),"gate".into()));
                runner.next_expiry=crate::engine::expiry::Expiry::from(Some(9000));
                let scope=runner.next_expiry.scope();
                assert!(runner.authorize_read("gates",&gate,false).unwrap());
                scope.emit();
                assert_eq!(runner.next_expiry.get(),Some(if rule==witness {until} else if rule.contains(".backup") {1200} else {9000}),"{rule}");
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Developer-owned, in-memory refusal context. Never part of Refusal or receipts.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#2.1 — only a host opt-in can collect the private refusing clause.
use super::runner::Runner;
use crate::{RuntimeError, schema::Effect};

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct RefusingClause {
    pub table: String,
    pub viewer: String,
    pub clause: String,
    pub phase: String,
    pub terminal: bool,
    pub no_candidate: bool,
    pub site: String,
    pub generation: u64,
}
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct RefusalTrace(std::sync::Arc<std::sync::Mutex<Option<RefusingClause>>>, std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64>);
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
impl RefusalTrace {
    /// Physical row images encountered and filtered by read authority in this request.
    pub fn hidden(&self) -> u64 { self.1.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) }
    pub fn take(&self) -> Option<RefusingClause> { self.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).take() }
}
impl Runner<'_> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.031#1.2 — only a real, uncapped storage miss; never replace a clause.
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn no_candidate(&self, table: &str, site: &str) {
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace {
            trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get_or_insert_with(|| RefusingClause {
                table: table.into(), viewer: self.context.viewer.clone(), clause: "no-candidate".into(), phase: "storage probe".into(),
                terminal: false, no_candidate: true, site: site.into(), generation: self.context.generation,
            });
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn refusing_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str) {
        self.refusing_phase(table, clause, clause);
    }
    pub(super) fn filtered_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str) {
        self.filtered_phase(table, clause, clause);
    }
    pub(super) fn filtered_phase(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str) {
        self.record_clause(table, clause, phase, false);
    }
    pub(super) fn refusing_phase(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str) {
        self.record_clause(table, clause, phase, true);
    }
    fn record_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str, terminal: bool) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace {
            *trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()) = Some(RefusingClause {
                table: table.into(), viewer: self.context.viewer.clone(), clause: clause.into(), phase: phase.into(), terminal, no_candidate: false, site: self.statement_site.clone(), generation: self.context.generation,
            });
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (table, clause, phase, terminal);
    }
    pub(super) fn trace_rule_result(&self, result: &Result<bool, RuntimeError>, table: &str, effect: Effect) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.is_some() {
            let phase = format!("{effect} rule");
            if matches!(result, Ok(false)) { self.record_clause(table, &phase, &phase, effect != Effect::Read); }
            self.trace_authorization(result, table, effect, &phase);
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (result, table, effect);
    }
    /// Preserve the phase before enclosing writes remap the public error effect.
    pub(super) fn trace_authorization<T>(&self, result: &Result<T, RuntimeError>, table: &str, effect: Effect, phase: &str) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.is_some() && matches!(result, Err(RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. })) {
            self.refusing_phase(table, &format!("authorization allowance ({effect})"), phase);
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (result, table, effect, phase);
    }
    pub(super) fn trace_unlocated_budget(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.as_ref().is_some_and(|trace| trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e|e.into_inner()).as_ref().is_none_or(|cause| !cause.clause.starts_with("authorization allowance"))) {
            self.refusing_phase(table, &format!("authorization allowance ({effect})"), "authorization access");
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (table, effect);
    }
    pub(super) fn hidden_row(&self) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace { trace.1.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed); }
    }

        let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_add(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read));

            )>(limit))?; }
        let mut cursor = bounds.clone();
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(limit);
        for _ in 0..limit {
            let candidate = self.reserved_scratch(bytes, &[], !trusted, |runner, scope| {
                loop {
                #[cfg(not(feature = "server-search"))]
                let selected = runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,1)?;
                #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
                let selected = if let Some(fields) = fields { runner.tx.scan_selected(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,fields)? } else { runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,1)? };
                let Some((row,key)) = selected.into_iter().next() else { return Ok(None); };
                // Advancing over a rejected candidate is internal decoder work.
                cursor.after = crate::key::decode(&key).map(|v|v.into_iter().skip(prefix.len()).collect());
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.6 — an expired, unswept candidate is never returned, counted or charged.
                if runner.step_inactive(name, &row, &key)? { continue; }
                return Ok(Some((runner.candidate_verdict(scope, name, index, prefix, row, trusted, rule_prepaid)?,key,
                    )));
                }
            })?;
            let Some((row,key)) = candidate else { break; };
            // Only this sealed surviving projection can be priced on the caller ledger.
            if metered && let Some(row) = &row {
                self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes() + row.projection_heap() + row.materialization_heap()
                    + crate::rows::word_heap::key_decode(table.index(index).map_or(1,|i|i.components.len()+1),key.len()))?;
            }
            out.push((row,key));
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// A system read of an expiring table takes the store path, so the store
    /// would price every fetched entry. Open the authored range once, step
    /// over expired entries internally, and price live entries through the
    /// same canonical-image codec used by every store.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.6 — the public meter charges only rows live at the read's clock.
    #[cfg_attr(not(feature = "sqlite"), allow(dead_code))]
    fn live_store_scan(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize, metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut cursor = bounds.clone();
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        // Price the authored range once. Internal cursors over expired keys
        // cannot select another public scan-opening charge or wider bounds.
        let mut guard = super::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut self.meter, |meter, scope| {
            if metered { crate::rows::word_heap::scan_open(meter, name, index, prefix, bounds, scope)?; }
            Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(crate::rows::word_heap::StoreGuard::open(meter, super::read::EXPIRED_STEP_GUARD))
        })?;
        while out.len() < limit {
            let window = self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
                runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,limit - out.len())
            })?;
            if window.is_empty() { break; }
            for (row, key) in window {
                cursor.after = crate::key::decode(&key).map(|v|v.into_iter().skip(prefix.len()).collect());
                if self.step_inactive(name, &row, &key)? { continue; }
                if metered {
                    let additional = super::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut self.meter, |meter, _| {
                        crate::rows::word_heap::scan_value_entry(meter, &mut guard, out.len(), out.capacity(), &row, &key)
                    })?;
                    if additional != 0 { out.reserve_exact(additional); }
                }
                out.push((row, key));
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// One fetched candidate's expiry, rule and field verdict, inside an
    /// internal scope. Unadmitted rule work is an unknown fact on a device.
    fn candidate_verdict(&mut self, scope: &super::authorized_scan::Authorization, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], mut row: Value, trusted: bool, rule_prepaid: bool) -> Result<Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
        let provisional = self.is_provisional(name,&row)?;
        let needs_rule = !self.context.is_system() && (!trusted || provisional);
        if needs_rule && !rule_prepaid && !self.device_work_active && !scope.admit_probes(super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read)) {
            // @ref LLP 3000.020#3.2 — unresolved authority is unknown, not an absent contribution.
            if self.device { self.unknown_fact(name, index, prefix, "source rule admission"); }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if trusted && !provisional && self.schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| m.parent_key.is_some()) {
            return Ok(Some(self.authorized_synthetic(table, row)));
        }
        self.authorize_image(name,row)
    }

    /// Cold certification has already admitted the member and entire source
    /// group, including a decoder page reserved before entering prepaid work. Every row
    /// still receives the ordinary expiry, rule and field verdict below.
    // @ref LLP 3000.015#3 — live count certification shares its transaction and authority.
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn scan_acquisition_basis(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        const PAGE: usize = 32;
        assert!(!self.device);
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        let rows = self.disclosed_scratch(0, &[], |runner, scope| {
            let selected = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, index, prefix, bounds, crate::rows::Dir::Asc, PAGE)?;
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(selected.len());
            for (mut row, key) in selected {
                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                let provisional = runner.is_provisional(name, &row)?;
                if provisional && !runner.context.is_system() && !scope.admit_probes(super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read)) {
                    out.push((None, key)); continue;
                }
                let row = if !provisional && runner.schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| m.parent_key.is_some()) {
                    Some(runner.authorized_synthetic(table, row))
                } else { runner.authorize_image(name, row)? };
                out.push((row, key));
            }
            Ok(out)
        })?;
        for (row, key) in &rows {
            if let Some(row) = row {
                self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes() + row.projection_heap() + row.materialization_heap()
                    + crate::rows::word_heap::key_decode(table.index(index).map_or(1, |i| i.components.len() + 1), key.len()))?;

//! Referential actions (LLP 3000.039): each reference between ordinary durable
//! tables says what deleting its target does — `on delete restrict` (the
//! default), `cascade`, `set null` or `keep`. This pass synthesizes the index
//! a reference is probed through, lowers the action into the schema, refuses
//! what a column cannot carry, checks that a delete's cascade fits its
//! program, and names every delete whose behavior the restrict default changed.
// @ref LLP 3000.039#2 — restrict is the default; cascade and set null need a cap on the reference.
use super::catalog::Catalog;
use super::intent_walk as walk;
use crate::{ast::*, diag::Diagnostic};
use snapback4_core::schema::{self as core, references::{self, OnDelete as Action}};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

fn action(declared: OnDelete) -> Action {
    match declared { OnDelete::Restrict => Action::Restrict, OnDelete::Cascade => Action::Cascade, OnDelete::SetNull => Action::SetNull, OnDelete::Keep => Action::Keep }
}

/// Before the schema is bound: every reference whose action probes its
/// children gets an index leading with the reference (then the child's
/// `expire at` column), unless one already exists. A `cap N by <column>` is
/// served by the same index.
pub(super) fn indexes(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    let ordinary = |catalog: &Catalog, name: &str| catalog.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|info| info.maintained.is_none() && info.decl.ephemeral.is_none());
    let mut wanted = Vec::new();
    for (name, info) in &catalog.tables {
        if !ordinary(catalog, name) { continue; }
        for column in &info.decl.columns {
            let TypeKind::Ref { table: parent } = &column.type_.kind else { continue };
            if column.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(a, _)| *a == OnDelete::Keep) || column.written_by.is_some() || !ordinary(catalog, parent) { continue; }
            if info.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|through| through.reference == column.name) { continue; }
            // @ref LLP 3000.039#2.3 — the effective deadline: a read-through child's `$deadline`, else `expire at`.
            let mut leading = vec![column.name.clone()];
            if info.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring) { leading.push(snapback4_core::schema::through::DEADLINE.to_owned()); }
            else if let Some((expire, _)) = &info.decl.expire { leading.push(expire.clone()); }
            if info.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.starts_with(&leading)) { continue; }
            leading.push("id".into());
            wanted.push((name.clone(), IndexDecl { name: references::reference_index_name(&column.name), columns: leading, unique: false, refusal: None, words: None, computed: BTreeMap::new(), site: column.site.clone() }));
        }
    }
    for (table, index) in wanted {
        catalog.synthesized.insert((table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
        catalog.tables.get_mut(&table).expect("catalog table").indexes.push(index);
    }
}

/// After receipts and timelines are bound (they decide which tables are

    /// Diagnostic recipe price: action work plus the root's ordinary write.
    /// Execution retains its existing separate charges for these two terms.

        let mut total = self.delete_reservation(parent)?;
        add(&mut total, self.unit_write(parent)?);
        Some(total)
    }

    /// Event images remain live until commit, after action scratch is released.

    // @ref LLP 3000.039#5.1 — two event images per priced row write, plus bounded keys and audience transitions, retained for the request lifetime.
    fn retained_action_heap(&self, parent: &str, writes: u64) -> Option<u64> {

                }
            }
        }

    }

//! Advisory proof of a child owner copied and checked against a stable parent.
use super::catalog::Catalog;
use crate::ast::{self, Expr, ReadExpr, ShapeField};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, Table, Constraint, ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
fn stable(table: &Table, fields: &[&str]) -> bool {
    if fields.iter().any(|field|table.written_by.contains_key(*field)||table.maintained.contains_key(*field)) { return false; }
    matches!(table.rules.update, RuleExpr::Deny) || fields.iter().all(|field| table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Immutable { fields } if fields.iter().any(|f| f == field))))
}
fn conjuncts(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> { match rule { RuleExpr::And(rs) => rs.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(), r => vec![r] } }
fn equality(rule: &RuleExpr, a: &RuleTerm, b: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    conjuncts(rule).iter().any(|r| matches!(r, RuleExpr::Eq(x,y) if x == a && y == b || x == b && y == a))
}
fn rename(rule: &mut RuleExpr, child: &str, parent: &str) {
    let term = |t: &mut RuleTerm| if matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == child) { *t = RuleTerm::Field(parent.into()); };
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { term(a); term(b); }
        RuleExpr::And(rs) | RuleExpr::Or(rs) => for r in rs { rename(r,child,parent); },
        RuleExpr::Not(r) => rename(r,child,parent),
        RuleExpr::Exists { key,equalities,.. } | RuleExpr::Created { key,equalities,.. } => { for k in key { term(k); } for (_,v) in equalities { term(v); } },
        _ => {}
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#7 — no application assertion alone verifies stored child ownership.
pub(super) fn readable(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, read: &ReadExpr, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    let Some(child) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false; };
    if schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,&read.table) { return false; }
    for part in &read.keys {
        let Some(reference) = &part.column else { continue; };
        let Some(ColumnType::Ref { table: parent_name }) = child.columns.get(reference) else { continue; };
        let Some(parent) = schema.table(parent_name) else { continue; };
        // Immutable owner fields do not prevent a kept id being deleted and recreated.
        if child.on_delete.get(reference).is_none_or(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Keep)
            && (!matches!(parent.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny) || parent.expire.is_some() || parent.ephemeral.is_some()
                || parent.on_delete.values().any(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade)) { continue; }
        if schema.maintain_for(parent_name).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,parent_name) { continue; }
        let input = crate::print::expression(&part.value);
        let known_parent = known.contains(&format!("exists {parent_name}[id = {input}]"));
        let projected_parent = matches!(&part.value, Expr::Member { base,name,.. } if name == "id" && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal { .. }))
            && projected_from(catalog, read, parent_name);
        if !known_parent && !projected_parent { continue; }
        for (owner, kind) in &child.columns {
            if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(child, &[reference,owner]) { continue; }
            for (parent_owner, kind) in &parent.columns {
                if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(parent, &[parent_owner]) { continue; }
                let a = RuleTerm::Field(owner.clone());
                let b = RuleTerm::Reference { table: parent_name.clone(), column: reference.clone(), field: parent_owner.clone(), old:false };
                let verified = child.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().all(|branch| equality(branch,&a,&b)
                    || equality(branch,&a,&RuleTerm::Viewer) && equality(branch,&b,&RuleTerm::Viewer));
                if !verified || matches!(child.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) { continue; }
                if child.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=owner)) || parent.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=parent_owner)) { continue; }
                let mut readers = child.rules.read.clone(); rename(&mut readers,owner,parent_owner);
                if readers == parent.rules.read { return true; }
            }
        }
    }
    false
}
fn system_writes(catalog:&Catalog,table:&str)->bool {
    catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ast::ProgramKind::Job).any(|p| {
        if p.native { return true; }
        let mut changed=false;let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
        super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Write { table:t,.. } if t==table) || matches!(e,Expr::Call { .. }) { changed=true; });
        changed
    })
}
fn projected_from(catalog: &Catalog, wanted: &ReadExpr, parent: &str) -> bool {
    let mut found = false;
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body = p.body.clone();
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |expr| {
                if let Expr::Shape { base,fields,.. } = expr && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Read(r) if r.table == parent) {
                    // Only the direct field's probe has this focal row. Nested shapes do not.
                    found |= fields.iter().any(|f| matches!(f,ShapeField::Field { value:Expr::Exists { read,.. },.. } if read.site == wanted.site));
                }
            });
        }
    }
    found
}
/// Positional typed table arguments denote id, even beside other unique keys.
pub(super) fn typed_id(catalog: &Catalog, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    let [part] = read.keys.as_slice() else { return false; };
    let Expr::Name { name,.. } = &part.value else { return false; };
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for p in &doc.programs {
            if !p.args.iter().any(|a| a.name == *name && matches!(&a.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref { table } if table == &read.table)) { continue; }
            let mut seen = false; let mut body=p.body.clone();
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Read(r) if r.site == read.site) { seen=true; });
            if seen { return true; }
        }
    }
    false

    pub(super) fn note_expiry(&mut self, table: &Table, row: &Value) {
        if let Some(column) = table.expiry_column()
            && let Ok(Value::Int(at)) = row.field(column)
            && self.context.now.is_some_and(|now| *at > now)
        {
            self.next_expiry.note(*at);
        }
    }

    /// Delivered rows are authoritative except when a temporary audience
    /// can end on the device's clock without a server commit. Retained
    /// history stays readable; until-revoked content rechecks that audience.
    pub(super) fn trust_synced_read(&self, table: &Table) -> bool {
        self.device && table.sync.as_ref().is_some_and(|sync| {
            table.leave == crate::schema::LeavePolicy::DeliveredHistory || match &sync.audience {
                crate::schema::Audience::Target { table, .. } => self.schema.table(table).is_none_or(|target| !target.read_lifetime_sensitive()),
                _ => true,
            }
        })
    }

    /// Whether the viewer may `effect` the row. The system principal is the
    /// store's owner and passes every rule; validation and constraints
    /// still run for it.
    pub(crate) fn check_rule(&mut self, table_name: &str, effect: Effect, old: Option<&Value>, next: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if effect != Effect::Read && self.timeline_may_write(table_name, effect) { return Ok(true); }
        if self.context.is_system() {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        if effect == Effect::Read && self.is_provisional(table_name, next)? { return self.provisional_readable(table_name, next); }
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        // A device holds a synced table's rows because the server delivered
        // each under the read rule and withdraws what it revokes; what
        // `retain delivered-history` kept stays readable here (LLP 3000 §7).
        if effect == Effect::Read && self.trust_synced_read(table) {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7(a): an unnamed update rule judges old and next.
        let mut rule = table.rules.for_effect(effect).clone();
        if effect == Effect::Update && old.is_some() && table.judges_old_image() { rule = rule.old_and_next(); }
        if effect == Effect::Update && let Some(old) = old {
            for (field, authority) in &table.written_by {
                if !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())) {
                    rule.fold_untouched_field(field, &table.columns[field]);
                }
            }
        }
        if effect == Effect::Read { self.rule_dependencies(&rule); }
        let bytes = self.rule_scratch(&rule);
        let result = self.authorized_scratch(bytes, &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes, rules::rule_probes(&rule))], |runner, allowance| {
            let mut next = next.clone();
            materialize_optional_fields(&mut next, table);
            let old = old.map(|old| {
                let mut old = old.clone();
                materialize_optional_fields(&mut old, table);
                old
            });
            runner.evaluate_sequence_rule(allowance, table_name, &rule, effect, old.as_ref(), &next)
        }).map_err(|error| error.authorization_context(table_name, effect));
        self.trace_rule_result(&result, table_name, effect);
        result
    }

    /// A row by id, materialized, without a rule check.
    pub(crate) fn fetch(&mut self, _allowance: &Authorization, table_name: &str, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
        self.schema.refuse_private(table_name)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        let found = if crate::rows::disclosed::actual_size(table) {
            self.live_lookup(_allowance, table_name, "byId", &table.indexes["byId"], &[Value::Id(id.into())], #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] None)?
        } else { self.authorized_scratch(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table), &[], |runner, authority| {
            runner.tx.get(authority, table_name, id).map(|found| {
                        found.map(|mut row| {
                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                row
            })
                    })
        })? };
        if self.device && (table.sync.is_none() || (found.is_none() && table.sync.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.horizon.is_some()))) { self.unknown_fact(table_name, "byId", &[Value::Id(id.to_owned())], "write target"); }
        match found {
            Some(row) if !self.inactive(table_name, &row)? => Ok(Some(row)),
            _ => Ok(None),
        }
    }
